               International Monetary Fund
              </a>
              projections

           </figcaption>
          </figure>
          <p id="mwRw">
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gross_domestic_product" id="mwSA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Gross domestic product">
            Gross domestic product
           </a>
           (GDP) is the
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Market_value" id="mwSQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Market value">
            market value
           </a>
           of all final goods and services from a nation in a given year.
           <sup about="#mwt41" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-4"}}' id="cite_ref-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref">
            <a href="#cite_note-4" id="mwSg">
             <span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwSw">
              <span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTA">
               [
              </span>
              2
              <span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTQ">
               ]
              </span>
             </span>
            </a>
           </sup>
           Countries are sorted by nominal GDP estimates from financial and statistical institutions, which are calculated at market or government official
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Exchange_rate" id="mwTg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Exchange rate">
            exchange rates
           </a>
           . Nominal GDP does not take into account differences in the
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cost_of_living" id="mwTw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Cost of living">
            cost of living
           </a>
           in different countries, and the results can vary greatly from one year to another based on fluctuations in the exchange rates of the country's
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Currency" id="mwUA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Currency">
            currency
           </a>
           .
           <sup about="#mwt44" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-5"}}' id="cite_ref-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref">
            <a href="#cite_note-5" id="mwUQ">
             <span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwUg">
              <span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUw">
               [
              </span>
              3
              <span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVA">
               ]
              </span>
             </span>
            </a>
           </sup>
           Such fluctuations may change a country's ranking from one year to the next, even though they often make little or no difference in the standard of living of its population.
           <sup about="#mwt47" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-6"}}' id="cite_ref-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref">
            <a href="#cite_note-6" id="mwVQ">
             <span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwVg">
              <span class="cite-bracket" id="mwVw">
               [
              </span>
              4
              <span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWA">
               ]
              </span>
             </span>
            </a>
           </sup>
          </p>
          <p id="mwWQ">
           Comparisons of national wealth are also frequently made based on
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Purchasing_power_parity" id="mwWg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Purchasing power parity">
            purchasing power parity
           </a>
           (PPP), to adjust for differences in the cost of living in different countries. Other metrics,
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_countries_by_GDP_(nominal)_per_capita" id="mwWw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="List of countries by GDP (nominal) per capita">
            nominal GDP per capita
           </a>
           and a corresponding
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_countries_by_GDP_(PPP)_per_capita" id="mwXA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="List of countries by GDP (PPP) per capita">
            GDP (PPP) per capita
           </a>
           , are used for comparing national
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Standard_of_living" id="mwXQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Standard of living">
            standard of living
           </a>
           . On the whole, PPP per capita figures are less spread than nominal GDP per capita figures.
           <sup about="#mwt50" class="mw-ref reference" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-7"}}' id="cite_ref-7" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref">
            <a href="#cite_note-7" id="mwXg">
             <span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwXw">
              <span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYA">
               [
              </span>
              5
              <span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYQ">
               ]
              </span>
             </span>
            </a>
           </sup>
          </p>
          <p id="mwYg">
           The rankings of national economies have changed significantly over time. For instance, the United States overtook the British Empire around 1916; Japan rose rapidly in the post-World War II period to become the world’s second-largest economy by the 1970s; China moved from ninth place in 1978 to second in 2010 following market reforms. These shifts reflect long-term changes in global economic output.
          </p>
          <p id="mwYw">
           The first list includes estimates compiled by the
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_Monetary_Fund" id="mwZA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="International Monetary Fund">
            International Monetary Fund
           </a>
           's World Economic Outlook, the second list shows the
           <a class="mw-redirect" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Bank" id="mwZQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="World Bank">
            World Bank
           </a>
           's data, and the third list includes data compiled by the
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Statistics_Division" id="mwZg" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="United Nations Statistics Division">
            United Nations Statistics Division
           </a>
           . The IMF's definitive data for the past year and estimates for the current year are published twice a year in April and October. Non-sovereign entities (the world, continents, and some
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dependent_territory" id="mwZw" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Dependent territory">
            dependent territories
           </a>
           ) and states with limited international recognition (such as
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kosovo" id="mwaA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Kosovo">
            Kosovo
           </a>
           and
           <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Taiwan" id="mwaQ" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="Taiwan">
            Taiwan
           </a>
           ) are included in the list where they appear in the sources.
          </p>
          <!-- These are marked in ''italics''.-->

         </section>

          </p>

         <section aria-labelledby="Nominal_GDP_of_regional_groupings" data-mw-section-id="2" id="mwBRU">
          <div class="mw-heading mw-heading2">
           <h2 id="Nominal_GDP_of_regional_groupings">
            Nominal GDP of regional groupings
           </h2>
          </div>
          <p id="mwBRY">
           In addition to the national GDP values stated in the previous section, the IMF and World Bank statistics include GDP estimates for various regional grouping and trade blocks. The table below provides a list of these groupings, including the 13 IMF regions and, where available, the equivalent value from World Bank sources.
           <span about="#mwt391" class="mw-empty-elt" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"sticky header","href":"./Template:Sticky_header"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}' id="mwBRc" typeof="mw:Transclusion">
            <link about="#mwt392" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"template:sticky header/styles.css"}}' href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1305414688" rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles"/>
           </span>
           <span about="#mwt393" class="mw-empty-elt" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"table alignment","href":"./Template:Table_alignment"},"params":{},"i":0}}]}' id="mwBRg" typeof="mw:Transclusion">
            <link about="#mwt394" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:Table alignment/styles.css"}}' href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1349610755" rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles"/>
           </span>
          </p>
          <table about="#mwt396" class="wikitable sortable sticky-header-multi static-row-numbers" data-mw="{&quot;attribs&quot;:[[{&quot;txt&quot;:&quot;style&quot;,&quot;html&quot;:&quot;&lt;span about=\&quot;#mwt395\&quot; typeof=\&quot;mw:Transclusion\&quot; id=\&quot;mwBRk\&quot; data-mw='{\&quot;parts\&quot;:[{\&quot;template\&quot;:{\&quot;target\&quot;:{\&quot;wt\&quot;:\&quot;right\&quot;,\&quot;href\&quot;:\&quot;./Template:Right\&quot;},\&quot;params\&quot;:{},\&quot;i\&quot;:0}}]}'&gt;style=\&quot;text-align:right\&quot;|&lt;/span&gt;&quot;},{&quot;html&quot;:&quot;&quot;}]]}" id="mwBRo" style="text-align:right" typeof="mw:ExpandedAttrs">

                </span>
               </a>
              </span>
              <span class="mw-reference-text reference-text" data-mw-group="n" id="mw-reference-text-cite_note-3">
               Based on the best available GDP figure for each country at the time of creation (14 april 2026). Best available GDP figure was defined as the latest available figure from the
               <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_Monetary_Fund" id="mwBd8" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="International Monetary Fund">
                International Monetary Fund
               </a>
               (including forecasts for the current year), otherwise the latest available figure from the
               <a class="mw-redirect" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Bank" id="mwBeA" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="World Bank">
                World Bank
               </a>
               , otherwise the latest available figure from the
               <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations_Statistics_Division" id="mwBeE" rel="mw:WikiLink" title="United Nations Statistics Division">
                United Nations Statistics Division
               </a>
               ; however, countries that didn't have any figure for a year more recent than 2026 were not included at all. For almost all countries, the latest IMF forecast figure for 2026 was used.
              </span>
